# Counts and residues

COMPUTED, T4, unreviewed. Exact integers from `code/count.py --max 16`. The corpus has 297 plantri graphs through 16 vertices, with every one of their 7,868 facial roots, plus the two larger named graphs and all their 72 roots, and all 30 roots of the saved 17-vertex restart fixture. Total: 300 graph records and 7,970 roots. No exhaustive enumeration beyond order 16 is claimed. `counts.json` stores edges, roots, mate histograms, all residues, and every primal edge avoidance count.

Commands from repository root:

```powershell
python -B research/2026-11-14_sol_route-algebra/code/count.py --max 16
python -B research/2026-11-14_sol_route-algebra/code/invariants.py
python -B research/2026-11-14_sol_route-algebra/code/check.py
python -B research/2026-11-14_sol_route-algebra/code/tables.py
```

## Specific failures

COMPUTED. Smallest here means smallest in this corpus, not an unrestricted minimality theorem.

- Odd tree count and signed count one fail on the octahedron, with T=2 and signed count zero.
- Universal nonzero tree count mod 3 fails at plantri_13_7, outer face 2, with S=12, P=8, T=6.
- Universal nonzero tree count mod 4 fails at plantri_9_0, with T=4 at its unobstructed roots.
- Universal nonzero tree count mod 5 fails at a 14-vertex rooted example, as the full table records.
- Universal nonzero tree count mod 7 fails at plantri_16_27, outer 19, with S=26, P=18, T=14.
- Universal nonzero tree count mod 11 fails on the named truncated cuboctahedron, outer 0, with S=384, P=22, T=22.
- Unrooted invariant equality fails on plantri_12_2, whose tree counts vary between 2 and 8 with the root even though all roots admit pairs.
- All unweighted signed counts vanish. Positive existence certificates must retain a statistic or select another coefficient.

## Independent small checks

COMPUTED. `code/check.py` uses all permutations for states, disjoint-set cycle detection after root contraction for trees, and path DFS for Hamiltonian cycles and avoidance. See `checks.json` for its completed range. This is a second implementation by the author, not an independent referee.

## Invariant comparisons

COMPUTED. `invariants.json` gives full Tutte polynomials of all three two-color vertex graphs, and all six hypergraph interior/exterior polynomials, for all five plantri graphs of orders 6,8,9,10. Interior and exterior coefficients are dictionaries indexed by inactivity exponent. Their evaluations at one equal S in these data.

| Example | T | S | Three graph T(1,1) values | Six hypergraph I(1)=X(1) values | Tait / Penrose P_G(3) | Z3 flows on primal G |
|---|---:|---:|---|---|---:|---:|
| plantri_6_0 | 2 | 2 | 4,4,4 | 2,2,2,2,2,2 | 24 | 2 |
| plantri_8_0 | 2 | 3 | 12,12,6 | 3,3,3,3,3,3 | 72 | 2 |
| plantri_9_0 | 4 | 4 | 15,15,15 | 4,4,4,4,4,4 | 96 | 2 |
| plantri_10_0 | 2 | 4 | 32,32,8 | 4,4,4,4,4,4 | 264 | 2 |
| plantri_10_1 | 2 | 5 | 20,36,20 | 5,5,5,5,5,5 | 168 | 2 |

LIT. The interpretation of Penrose P_G(3) as labeled Tait colorings is the standard plane cubic specialization. Source identification and access limits are in ROUTES.md. COMPUTED values here enumerate the colorings; they do not independently evaluate the Penrose state sum. The Z3 flow value follows from PROOF 2, not a flow enumeration. No root-normalized or coefficient-extracted invariant identity has been excluded.
